### figure8.png

---
DIMENSIONS:     1832 x 1683 px — recommended **double column** (181 mm / 7.16 in) — **256 DPI** at that width (1832/7.16). For reference: 523 DPI if forced to single column (88 mm), and 267 DPI at the 6.875-in legacy double-column measure. Aspect ratio 1.09:1 → printed height ≈ 167 mm at double-column width, within the 230 mm limit. An 8-panel 4×2 layout with three spectrograms cannot legibly be reduced to 88 mm, so double column is the only viable choice.

RESOLUTION:     **Below threshold** — mixed figure (halftone spectrograms in c, e, g + line art in a, b, d, f, h); effective 256 DPI at double-column width vs. 300 DPI minimum for the halftone content and 600 DPI preferred for the line-art content.

FORMAT:         **Needs conversion** — PNG → TIFF (LZW) or, preferably, vector PDF/EPS for final submission. PNG is acceptable for review only.

FILE SIZE:      OK — 1.41 MB (limit 10 MB); ample headroom to re-export at 600 DPI.

TEXT RENDERING: **Rasterized** — all axis labels, tick labels, panel titles and the (f) legend are bitmap glyphs. In a figure that is predominantly line art this is a blocking quality issue.

COLOR MODE:     OK (assumed RGB; not reported in metadata — please confirm). Journal requires RGB, so no CMYK conversion is planned. Note that if any CMYK proofing occurs, the viridis extremes (saturated yellow ~45 dB and deep blue-violet ~10 dB) and the orange trace in (b)/(f) are the most likely to shift; the blue multitaper trace and orange "Hann, then box" trace remain distinguishable in grayscale by line weight, which is good.

ISSUES:
  - [BLOCKING] Resolution 256 DPI at the recommended 181 mm width is below the 300 DPI halftone minimum and well below the 600 DPI line-art target.
  - [BLOCKING] Rasterized text throughout; PNG raster text will show pixel edges in the printed line-art panels.
  - [BLOCKING] Panel (h) x-axis label is truncated at the right edge: "multitaper minus Hann-then-box (d" — the unit "(dB)" is cut off. The figure canvas clips panel (h)'s label, and the (h) title "median |diff| 1.5 dB" sits above a panel whose axis is incomplete.
  - [ADVISORY] Format PNG must be converted before final submission.
  - [ADVISORY] Each panel carries an in-figure descriptive title ("scalp EEG, channel C4, common-average reference; seizure shaded", "Hann periodogram, 2-s windows: ν = 2", etc.). IEEE TBME asks that figure titles not appear inside the artwork. Short parameter annotations are usually tolerated, but the longer sentence-like titles in (a) and (g) read as titles and would be better moved into the caption.
  - [ADVISORY] Statistical reporting is partly complete: the caption states 599 windows and gives ν for each estimator (2, 6, 4.8, 7.7), which satisfies the degrees-of-freedom requirement for the noise comparison. However, panel (h) plots a distribution over "all pixels" without stating N. The y-axis reads "pixels" with counts to ~5000, so N is on the order of 10^5; state the exact number (599 windows × number of frequency bins ≤ 30 Hz) and the histogram bin width in the caption.
  - [ADVISORY] The spectrograms are clipped at the top of the colour scale (large saturated-yellow ictal regions in c, e, g reach the 45 dB ceiling), so the ictal dynamic range is not resolvable. The caption notes the scale is shared with Fig. eeg2, which justifies it, but state explicitly that values above 45 dB and below 10 dB are clipped.
  - [ADVISORY] Panel (d) has no legend and depends entirely on the legend in (f), which appears two rows below. Readers scanning (c)-(d) first will not know the four traces.
  - [ADVISORY] Smallest text (the four legend entries in (f)) measures ~7.5–8 pt at 181 mm reproduction — above the 6 pt floor but at the low end; it will lose legibility if the figure is ever reduced.
  - [ADVISORY] The colorbar sits between rows (e) and (g) and serves panels (c), (e) and (g); its physical separation from (c) may cause readers to assume it applies only to (e).

SUGGESTED FIXES:
  - Re-export from the plotting source as vector PDF or EPS with the spectrogram images embedded at ≥300 DPI (use `rasterized=True` on the pcolormesh/imshow artists only, keeping axes and text vector). Set the figure width to exactly 7.16 in (181 mm) in the plotting script so no scaling occurs at typesetting.
  - If a raster-only deliverable is required, re-render at 600 DPI for 181 mm: ≥4296 × 3946 px, saved as LZW-compressed TIFF, RGB. Expect ~5–8 MB, still under the 10 MB cap.
  - Fix the clipped (h) x-axis label: apply a tight bounding box / increase the right margin so "multitaper minus Hann-then-box (dB)" renders in full; consider shortening to "MT − Hann-box (dB)" with the expansion given in the caption.
  - Move the long in-panel titles of (a) and (g) into the caption; retain only compact parameter annotations inside the panels (e.g. "ν = 2", "NW = 2, K = 3", "W = 1 Hz").
  - Add N and bin width for panel (h), e.g. "(h) Distribution of the pixelwise difference (e) − (g) over all 599 × 61 = 36 539 time–frequency pixels, 0.25 dB bins; median |difference| 1.5 dB."
  - State clipping explicitly in the caption: "values outside 10–45 dB are clipped to the end colours."
  - Add an abbreviated legend or direct line labels to (d) (or a single shared legend placed above the (d)/(f) column), and extend the colorbar so it visually spans rows (c), (e) and (g), or add "scale shared with (c), (e), (g)" next to it.
  - Confirm the export color profile is RGB (sRGB) and keep the viridis map, which is perceptually uniform and grayscale-safe.

DIMENSIONS:     1922 × 1498 px — recommended **double column** (181 mm / 7.16 in) — **268 DPI** at that width

                Aspect ratio 1.28:1 → at 181 mm the printed height is ≈142 mm, well within the 230 mm limit. Aspect ratio is appropriate for a 4-panel stacked time-axis layout; single-column use is not viable (600 s of time axis across 88 mm would compress the spectrograms to ~2.5 mm/100 s and the in-panel titles would fall below 6 pt).

RESOLUTION:     **Below threshold** at the recommended width — figure is **mixed** (spectrogram images = halftone/continuous-tone; panel (a) trace, axes, ticks, text = line art). Actual 268 DPI < 300 DPI halftone minimum and far below the 600 DPI line-art preference.

FORMAT:         **Needs conversion** — PNG → TIFF (LZW) or, preferably, vector PDF/EPS with the four raster spectrogram/trace layers embedded. PNG is acceptable for review only.

FILE SIZE:      OK — 2.01 MB (limit 10 MB); a 600 DPI TIFF of this layout will land at roughly 8–10 MB, so use LZW compression or submit PDF/EPS.

TEXT RENDERING: **Rasterized** — all text (axis labels, tick labels, panel titles, panel letters, colourbar labels) is baked into the PNG bitmap. Estimated tick-label size at 181 mm is ~11 pt and titles ~12 pt, i.e. size is compliant, but the glyph edges are pixel-limited.

COLOR MODE:     OK (assumed RGB; metadata did not report colour mode — confirm). Viridis is perceptually uniform and prints acceptably, but the saturated yellow-green top of the scale and the pale peach seizure shading in (a) are the two CMYK-risk elements: the peach fill (very low saturation, high lightness) may nearly vanish in print or in grayscale conversion.

ISSUES:
  - [BLOCKING] Effective resolution 268 DPI at 181 mm is below the 300 DPI minimum for halftone content and well below 600 DPI for the line-art elements (panel (a) waveform, axis rules, text).
  - [BLOCKING] Text and line art are rasterized. For a graph/diagram figure IEEE TBME treats rasterized line art as a quality failure; the (a) trace at 268 DPI will also show aliasing in the dense high-amplitude bursts.
  - [BLOCKING] Format: PNG is not an accepted final-submission format; convert to TIFF/EPS/PDF.
  - [BLOCKING] Titles are placed inside the figure. Four in-panel title strings ("scalp EEG, channel Fp1, common-average reference; 2 seizures shaded", "Hann periodogram, 2-s windows: ν = 2", "multitaper, NW = 2, K = 3: ν = 6", "Hann periodogram, then box of half-width W = 1.0 Hz: ν = 4.8") function as titles and must be removed or reduced to short keyed annotations, with the descriptive content moved to the caption.
  - [ADVISORY] Panel (a) x-axis extent does not align with panels (b)–(d): its right spine ends ~55 px beyond theirs and it carries no tick labels, so the reader cannot verify that the trace and spectrograms share the same 0–600 s mapping. Align all four axes to a common x-range and bounding box.
  - [ADVISORY] The shared colourbar is vertically centred on panels (b)–(c) only, implying it does not apply to (d); its span or placement should make the "same colour scale" statement visually unambiguous.
  - [ADVISORY] Colourbar is labelled "power (dB)" with no reference quantity. State the units/reference (e.g. dB re 1 µV²/Hz) in the caption or axis label.
  - [ADVISORY] Statistical reporting is largely complete (599 windows, 2 s stepped by 1 s, ν = 2 / 6 / 4.8 for the three estimators) — this satisfies the "number of windows" and "degrees of freedom" requirements. Missing: the sampling rate / Nyquist and FFT length, and the derivation basis for ν = 4.8 (number of independent periodogram bins inside the 1 Hz box, accounting for 50 % window overlap). Add one sentence so the noise-level comparison between (b), (c) and (d) is reproducible.
  - [ADVISORY] Seizure shading in (a) relies on a single pale hue with no outline; it will be weak in print and invisible in grayscale.
  - [ADVISORY] Panel letters (a)–(d) are lowercase and parenthesised as required, but they sit at different horizontal offsets relative to their panels ((a) is inset left of the y-label, (b)–(d) sit above it). Place them consistently.

SUGGESTED FIXES:
  - Regenerate from source at exactly 181 mm width and export as vector PDF or EPS: keep the three spectrograms and the (a) trace as embedded rasters at ≥600 DPI (`rasterized=True` on the pcolormesh/imshow artists, `dpi=600`), and leave axes, ticks, text and the colourbar frame as vector. This fixes the resolution, text-rendering and format issues in one step.
  - If a raster-only deliverable is required, re-render the whole canvas at ≥4280 px wide (600 DPI × 7.16 in) and save as TIFF/LZW, RGB; do not upscale the existing 1922 px file.
  - Delete the four in-panel titles. Move their content to the caption, keyed to the panel letters, e.g. "(a) Fp1 vs. common average, clipped at ±500 µV, seizures shaded; (b) Hann periodogram, ν = 2; (c) multitaper NW = 2, K = 3, ν = 6; (d) Hann periodogram smoothed with a 1 Hz half-width box, ν = 4.8." If the ν values must remain visible on the plot, place them as short right-aligned annotations inside each spectrogram.
  - Share the x-axis across all four panels (`sharex=True`), keep tick marks on (a)–(c) without labels, and label only the bottom axis as now.
  - Extend or recentre the colourbar so it clearly serves panels (b)–(d), and add the dB reference to its label.
  - Add a darker outline (or hatch) to the seizure spans in (a) so they survive print and grayscale.
  - Add sampling rate, FFT length, and the counting argument behind ν = 4.8 to the caption; state explicitly that all three estimates use the identical 599 windows.
  - Confirm and record RGB colour mode in the exported file; do not convert to CMYK.

## Caption & Legend Auditor

### efigure1.png

---
TITLE:              Present & bold — "Kernels at the same design bandwidth"

PANEL DESCRIPTIONS: Complete — (a) linear scale near the main lobe, (b) dB relative to peak; order matches the figure.

ERROR BARS:         Not present — the figure shows deterministic analytic/numerical kernels, so no error bars or realization count are required. State this explicitly (see fixes) so a reviewer does not read the absence as an omission.

SAMPLE SIZE:        Stated in the sense that matters here ($N=256$ samples, $W=4/N$, $K=7$ tapers). No number of realizations is applicable because no random quantity is plotted — but the caption should say so.

STATISTICAL DETAIL: Partial — equivalent degrees of freedom are given numerically ($\nu = 14.0,\ 17.2,\ 8.9,\ 7.3$) satisfying the journal's "state the estimator's degrees of freedom" rule, but (i) the symbol $\nu$ is never defined in the caption, (ii) the basis for $\nu$ (e.g., $\nu = 2/\mathrm{Var}$ ratio, Welch–Satterthwaite equivalent d.o.f., or $\nu = 2K$ for multitaper) is not named, and (iii) no test is involved (correctly — no p-values needed).

ABBREVIATIONS:      Undefined: $\nu$ (equivalent degrees of freedom); "then box" (convolution with the rectangular smoother of half-width $W$ is implied but never stated); "Bohman" (taper family — acceptable if defined earlier in the text, otherwise add a one-clause gloss); "dB re peak" (axis shorthand, expand as "decibels relative to the kernel peak"). $N$, $W$, $K$ are adequately defined via the numeric values and the in-figure relation $K = 2NW-1$.

COLOR/SYMBOL KEY:   Present — in-figure legend maps the five curves (dotted black ideal box, dark blue multitaper, green untapered, orange Hann, light blue Bohman). The caption lists the same five estimators in the same order, which is good practice; consider adding the colors parenthetically for grayscale/colorblind readers since dark blue vs. light blue is the only cue separating multitaper from Bohman.

SELF-CONTAINED:     No — a reader cannot reconstruct (1) what quantity the ordinate is (kernel normalization: "units of N" and "dB re peak" need one clause each), (2) what operation "then box" denotes, (3) what $\nu$ means, or (4) where the "40 dB" skirt difference is evaluated.

ISSUES:
  - [BLOCKING] The symbol $\nu$ is used four times but never defined. Since the whole point of the figure is a bandwidth-matched comparison of estimators with different noise properties, the degrees-of-freedom definition must appear in the caption per the journal's noise-comparison requirement.
  - [BLOCKING] The caption does not state that the plotted curves are deterministic (analytically/numerically computed) kernels rather than estimates from data. Without this, the missing error bars and missing realization count are unresolvable ambiguities for a reviewer applying the error-bar rule.
  - [BLOCKING] "then box" is unexplained. The reader cannot tell that the periodogram kernel is convolved with a rectangular (boxcar) smoothing window of half-width $W$ to equalize bandwidth.
  - [ADVISORY] Kernel normalization is unstated. Panel (a) ordinate is "kernel, linear (units of N)" — say explicitly that kernels are normalized to unit area (so the plotted height scales as $N$), otherwise the 0.125 plateau is unmotivated.
  - [ADVISORY] "the skirts differ by 40 dB" is imprecise: no frequency offset is given. From panel (b) the untapered-vs-Bohman gap is roughly 50–60 dB at $|\Delta f| \approx 10/N$, so the number should be tied to a stated offset.
  - [ADVISORY] Caption cites Table~\ref{tab:dof} for the same $\nu$ values that are already in the caption; either drop the duplicate numbers or drop the cross-reference so the caption stands alone (a caption should not depend on a table for completeness).
  - [ADVISORY] Dark blue (multitaper) and light blue (Bohman) are distinguishable only by lightness; in grayscale print or for deuteranopic readers these two will merge. The dotted black box is fine.
  - [ADVISORY] Layout: roughly the central third of the canvas is empty, and the legend sits under panel (b) only, visually implying it applies to (b) alone. At 181 mm double-column width the panels will be smaller than necessary.
  - [ADVISORY] "Hann" and "Bohman" tapers, and the multitaper relation $K = 2NW-1$, appear only inside the image. Axis/legend text must be ≥6 pt at final size; verify after any rescaling, and confirm the file is supplied at ≥600 DPI (or as vector EPS/PDF) for final submission — a 2172 × 616 px raster at 181 mm is only ~305 DPI, which meets the color line-art minimum but not the preferred 600 DPI.
  - [ADVISORY] Panel labels are lowercase and bold as required; no in-figure title is present. Both compliant — no action.

SUGGESTED FIXES:
  - Revised caption: "\textbf{Kernels at the same design bandwidth.} Smoothing kernels computed analytically for $N = 256$ samples and design half-bandwidth $W = 4/N$, shown (a) on a linear ordinate near the main lobe and (b) in decibels relative to the kernel peak. Curves are deterministic kernels, not estimates from data; no averaging over realizations is involved, hence no error bars. All kernels are normalized to unit area, so linear amplitude is expressed in units of $N$. Curves: ideal rectangular box of half-width $W$ (black dotted); Sine/Slepian multitaper kernel with $K = 2NW - 1 = 7$ tapers (dark blue); and the untapered (green), Hann (orange) and Bohman (light blue) periodograms each convolved with the rectangular box of half-width $W$ so that all five kernels share the same design bandwidth. Numbers in parentheses are the equivalent (Welch–Satterthwaite) degrees of freedom $\nu = 2\left(\sum_j w_j\right)^2 / \sum_j w_j^2$ of the corresponding spectral estimator at one frequency: multitaper $\nu = 14.0$, untapered $\nu = 17.2$, Hann $\nu = 8.9$, Bohman $\nu = 7.3$. Main-lobe widths agree to within X\%, whereas sidelobe (skirt) levels at $|\Delta f| = 10/N$ span about Y~dB, from $-$Z~dB (untapered) to $-$Q~dB (Bohman). No statistical tests are performed in this figure."
  - Fill in X, Y, Z, Q from the plotted data; do not leave the qualitative "40 dB" claim unanchored to a frequency offset.
  - Add the color names to the caption text (as above) so the figure survives grayscale reproduction, and consider changing the Bohman curve to a distinct dash pattern rather than a lighter blue.
  - Replace the duplicated $\nu$ values + Table cross-reference with either the caption numbers alone or "see Table~\ref{tab:dof} for the full list"; do not do both.
  - Re-lay out as a two-panel double-column figure with the panels expanded to fill the width and the shared legend either centered beneath both panels with a clear "applies to both panels" position or moved into panel (b)'s empty upper-left region; this recovers the wasted central whitespace.
  - Supply the final file as vector PDF/EPS (or 600 DPI TIFF), RGB, under 10 MB, with all text ≥6 pt at printed size.

  - [ADVISORY] Figure geometry: 2121 px wide is fine for 181 mm at 300 DPI (≈7.07 in), but line art at this journal is preferred at 600 DPI; confirm axis/legend text is ≥6 pt (≥8 pt preferred) at 181 mm final width, and supply TIFF/EPS/PDF for production rather than PNG. (a) Estimates of the power spectral density (PSD): raw periodogram (grey; equivalent degrees of freedom ν = 2); untapered periodogram smoothed with a rectangular ('box') kernel of width 2W spanning M = 9 frequency bins (green, ν = 17.2); equal-weight multitaper estimate with K = 7 tapers (solid blue, ν = 14) and K = 6 tapers, i.e. the seventh, leakiest taper dropped (dashed blue, ν = 12); Hann-tapered periodogram smoothed with the same box kernel (orange, ν = 8.9); and the true AR(4) PSD (thick black). (b) Each smoothed estimate minus the true PSD; the thin horizontal black line marks 0 dB (zero bias). At high frequencies the untapered box estimate is biased by ≈+18 dB, K = 7 by ≈+11 dB, K = 6 by ≈+X dB, and the Hann-plus-box estimate remains within ±Y dB of the truth. ν denotes equivalent degrees of freedom of each estimator; frequency is in cycles per sample."

TITLE:              Present & bold — "Variance, leakage and side-lobe level versus measured resolution"
PANEL DESCRIPTIONS: Complete — (a), (b), (c) all described in order, lowercase labels matching the figure
ERROR BARS:         Not present — correctly justified ("all curves are exact kernel and degrees-of-freedom computations (no sampling variability)"); this satisfies the journal's "number of realizations behind any error bar" rule
SAMPLE SIZE:        Partial — $N=256$ is given, but $N$ is never named (series length in samples?); Welch segment length / number of segments is not stated, and the number of swept bandwidth points per curve (visible as markers) is unstated
STATISTICAL DETAIL: Not applicable (deterministic computation) — however the journal's "state the estimator's degrees of freedom where noise levels are compared" requirement is met by panel (a); the *definition* of $\nu$ (e.g., $\nu = 2(\mathbb{E}\hat S)^2/\mathrm{Var}\,\hat S$, or the Welch–Satterthwaite form used) is missing
ABBREVIATIONS:      Undefined: $N$ (series length), $W$ (time–half-bandwidth parameter; $NW$ product), $K$ (number of tapers — implied but not stated), "box" (boxcar/rectangular smoothing kernel and its width rule), "dof" as it appears on the panel (a) axis, dB reference convention in panel (c) (relative to kernel peak?), "half-power width" (−3 dB full width of the spectral window, in units of $1/N$)
COLOR/SYMBOL KEY:   Present — eight-entry in-figure legend below the panels; colors and markers both distinguish methods (color-blind-safe redundancy is good)
SELF-CONTAINED:     No — reader cannot reproduce or fully interpret without the main text: $W$, $K$, box/Gaussian smoother widths, Welch segment length, the definition of half-power width and of $\nu$, and the meaning of the $8/N$ reference line are all deferred (the last via a \ref to Table~\ref{tab:dof})

ISSUES:
  - [BLOCKING] Panel (b) and (c) y-axis labels are clipped/overlapped by the neighboring panel's axis ("kernel power beyond 2× half-power half-width" is cut at the left edge by panel (a)'s axis area; "peak beyond 1.5× half-power half-width (dB)" is overrun by panel (b)). Text collision makes labels unreadable at print size.
  - [BLOCKING] Caption relies on an external cross-reference (Table~\ref{tab:dof}) to explain the dotted vertical line. An extended/supplementary figure caption must state the meaning directly: "dotted line: half-power width $=8/N$, the operating point used in the main text."
  - [BLOCKING] Key defining quantities are undefined in the caption: "half-power width (units of $1/N$)", equivalent degrees of freedom $\nu$, and the dB reference in (c). Without these, panels cannot be interpreted from the figure alone.
  - [ADVISORY] Resolution: 1888 px across a double-column (181 mm) figure ≈ 265 DPI, below the ≥300 DPI minimum (≥600 DPI preferred for line art). Supply vector EPS/PDF or re-render at ≥2140 px width.
  - [ADVISORY] Method parameterization not reported: the box smoother width, the Gaussian kernel standard deviation, the sine-taper count, and the Welch segment length/overlap window length. Reviewers cannot verify the equal-resolution matching claim.
  - [ADVISORY] Curves terminate at different maximum half-power widths (multitaper ends near 16/N, Welch extends to ~25/N). The reason (parameter sweep limits, $K\le$ some bound) should be stated so the truncation is not read as data loss.
  - [ADVISORY] Panel (b) and (c) measure closely related quantities with similar wording ("beyond 2×" vs "beyond 1.5×"); one sentence clarifying that (b) is integrated leakage power and (c) is the worst-case side-lobe/skirt peak would prevent confusion.
  - [ADVISORY] The $1.5\times$ and $2\times$ thresholds are arbitrary; state briefly why they were chosen.
  - [ADVISORY] Legend sits well below the panels with a large white gap; tightening this recovers vertical space and increases effective resolution.

SUGGESTED FIXES:
  - Fix layout: increase inter-panel horizontal spacing (or use `constrained_layout`/`tight_layout`) so the (b) and (c) y-axis labels are fully visible; consider shortening them to "leakage power beyond 2 HPHW" and "side-lobe peak beyond 1.5 HPHW (dB)" with HPHW defined in the caption.
  - Revised caption: "**Variance, leakage and side-lobe level versus measured resolution.** All quantities are computed exactly from the analytic spectral windows and degrees-of-freedom expressions for a series of length $N=256$ samples; no Monte-Carlo realizations are involved, so no error bars apply. For each method the bandwidth parameter is swept and results are plotted against the resulting half-power width (full width of the spectral window at half its peak, in units of $1/N$), so methods are compared at matched resolution. Methods: multitaper with $K=2NW-1$ and $K=2NW-2$ Slepian tapers ($NW$ = time–half-bandwidth product, $W$ swept over ... ) and with $K=$ ... sine tapers; untapered, Hann and Bohman periodograms smoothed by a boxcar (rectangular) moving average of width ... bins; a Hann periodogram smoothed by a Gaussian of standard deviation ... bins; Welch's method with Hann windows of length ... samples and 50% overlap ($\dots$ segments). (a) Equivalent degrees of freedom $\nu = 2(\mathbb{E}\hat S)^2/\mathrm{Var}\{\hat S\}$; higher $\nu$ means lower estimator variance. (b) Fraction of total spectral-window power falling beyond twice the half-power half-width (integrated leakage). (c) Largest spectral-window value beyond $1.5\times$ the half-power half-width, in dB relative to the window peak; for the Gaussian-smoothed and Welch kernels, which have no flat top, this is the main-lobe skirt rather than a discrete side lobe. Markers denote the swept parameter values ($n=$ ... points per curve); colors and marker shapes identify methods as in the legend. Dotted vertical line: half-power width $=8/N$, the operating point tabulated in Table~\ref{tab:dof}. dof, degrees of freedom."
  - Add the definition of "dof" to the caption or spell out "equivalent degrees of freedom $\nu$" on the panel (a) axis.
  - Export as vector PDF/EPS (or ≥600 DPI TIFF) at 181 mm width; verify all text is ≥6 pt (target 8–10 pt) at that final size.
  - State the dB reference explicitly on the (c) axis, e.g., "side-lobe peak (dB re window peak)".

### efigure4.png

---
TITLE:              Not bold / Not self-contained — the opening phrase is a cross-reference ("Fig.~\ref{fig:banks} in full."), not a descriptive bold title
PANEL DESCRIPTIONS: Partial — rows (a–d, e–h, i–l, m–p) are described, but the four *columns* are only defined by reference to another figure; no per-panel text for the individual methods
ERROR BARS:         Not present (all curves/bars are deterministic) — but this must be stated explicitly
SAMPLE SIZE:        Missing — the record length N used to generate the panels (apparently N = 256) and the bandwidth W / time–bandwidth product NW are never given numerically; K is given only for column 4
STATISTICAL DETAIL: Partial — the equivalent degrees of freedom ν is printed in (i)–(l) but never defined in the caption (estimator, formula, e.g. ν = 2(Σc_k)²/Σc_k²); no statement that no averaging over realizations is involved; no test is required, but the df definition is mandatory under the journal's noise-comparison rule
ABBREVIATIONS:      Undefined: Q (the bank's covariance/Gram matrix), W (half-bandwidth), 2W, N, L, K, ν, c_k, f′, σ, "eigen-taper", "eigen-weight ladder", "dB re peak", "bank"; Hann, Welch, Slepian, Thomson assumed known but should be cited once
COLOR/SYMBOL KEY:   Partial — in-figure legends cover k = 0…3 and f′ = 0/N…3/N, and the caption explains the shaded band, but (i) the seven unlabelled curves in (a), (ii) the per-column colour code carried into rows (i)–(p) (pink / orange / teal / blue = Welch / smoothed periodogram / sliding sinc / Thomson), and (iii) the meaning of the vertical offsets ("curves offset") are not explained
SELF-CONTAINED:     No — a reader cannot identify what the four columns are without the referenced figure, cannot recover N, W, or the definition of Q and ν, and cannot tell how kernel width and "% beyond 2W" were computed
ISSUES:
  - [BLOCKING] No bold, self-contained descriptive title; the caption begins with a cross-reference. A stand-alone figure must not depend on another figure's caption for its column definitions.
  - [BLOCKING] Column identities absent. Columns 1–4 (Welch / smoothed single periodogram / sliding-sinc / Thomson multitaper) must be named in this caption.
  - [BLOCKING] Error-bar / variability statement missing. State once that all traces are deterministic (analytic tapers and kernels), that no realizations or windows are averaged, and therefore no error bars are shown.
  - [BLOCKING] ν (equivalent degrees of freedom) shown in (i)–(l) is undefined. Journal policy requires the estimator's degrees of freedom be stated with its definition when noise levels/variance are compared across methods.
  - [BLOCKING] Key numerical parameters missing: N, the analysis half-bandwidth W (and NW), segment length L in samples, number of tapers/offsets per bank (K or number of bars) for each column.
  - [ADVISORY] Definition of "half-power width" (−3 dB full width?) and of "% power beyond 2W" (∫|K|²df outside |f| ≤ W divided by total?) not given; values 5.8/N–8.0/N cannot be reproduced.
  - [ADVISORY] Q is used without definition; the sentence about the Slepians being "one of many orthonormal bases of the span" is opaque without it.
  - [ADVISORY] Vertical offsets in rows 1–2 are unquantified and y-axes are unlabelled in absolute units; state "curves offset vertically for display; amplitude in arbitrary units."
  - [ADVISORY] In-figure panel headers act as titles inside the figure, which IEEE discourages; consider moving the descriptive text to the caption or shortening to short keys.
  - [ADVISORY] Panel labels (c) and (d) collide with the adjacent column header text; the small in-panel legend text in (b) and (d) appears at/below the 6 pt floor at 181 mm width.
  - [ADVISORY] Colour distinguishability: rows 1–2 use a viridis-like ramp for k = 0…3 in which k = 2 (teal) and k = 3 (green) are close; verify greyscale/CVD legibility or add line styles.
SUGGESTED FIXES:
  - Rewrite the opening as a bold stand-alone title, e.g.: "\textbf{Eigen-decomposition of four spectral-estimator taper banks.}"
  - Name the columns explicitly: "Columns (left to right): Welch averaging (Hann, L = N/4, 50\% overlap, 7 offsets); smoothing of one Hann periodogram (Gaussian kernel, σ = 3/N); a sinc window slid over every frequency offset (untapered/boxcar); Thomson multitaper (K = 7 Slepian tapers, equal weights)."
  - Add the parameter sentence: "All panels use N = 256 samples and half-bandwidth W = 4/N (NW = 4)."
  - Define Q and the ladder: "Q is the N×N covariance (Gram) matrix of the bank's effective quadratic form; (e)–(h) show its first four eigenvectors (eigen-tapers) and (i)–(l) its normalized eigenvalues c_k (Σc_k = 1), the 'eigen-weight ladder'."
  - Define the df: "ν = 2(Σ_k c_k)² / Σ_k c_k² is the equivalent number of chi-square degrees of freedom of the resulting spectral estimate; values are printed in (i)–(l)."
  - Add: "All curves are analytic/deterministic; no realizations are averaged and no error bars apply."
  - Define kernel metrics: "(m)–(p) show the effective smoothing kernel, 10 log10 |K(f)|² normalized to its peak; 'width' is the full width at half power (−3 dB) in units of 1/N, and '% beyond 2W' is the fraction of ∫|K|² df falling outside |f| ≤ W; shading marks |f| ≤ W."
  - Add the colour key: "Colours in (i)–(p) identify the method as in the column headings (pink, Welch; orange, smoothed periodogram; teal, sliding sinc; blue, Thomson). In (e)–(h) and (b), (d) line colours index taper order k = 0–3 / modulation frequency f′ = 0/N–3/N as per the in-panel legends. In (a) the seven traces are the same Hann window at its seven 50\%-overlapped offsets."
  - Add: "Curves in rows 1–2 are offset vertically for display; amplitude is in arbitrary units."
  - Nudge the (c) and (d) panel labels left/down to clear the column headers and raise all in-panel legend text to ≥7 pt at 181 mm; export as ≥600 DPI TIFF/EPS in RGB.

### figure1.png

---
TITLE:              Present & bold — "Three routes, one estimate." (concise and informative; no title is duplicated inside the artwork, as required)

PANEL DESCRIPTIONS: Complete — (a), (b), (c) described in order, lowercase panel labels match the figure

ERROR BARS:         Not present — but the legend never says so. Because panels (a)–(b) are single-realization deterministic comparisons, add one explicit sentence ("Single epoch; no error bars — the three routes are algebraically equivalent, so the curves are deterministic given the data.") Otherwise a reviewer will read the absence of uncertainty as an omission.

SAMPLE SIZE:        Partially stated — $N=1024$ samples, "one epoch" given for (a)/(b); missing for (c): the number of sliding windows averaged at each $L$, and how many epochs/channels contribute to each plotted point (one, presumably, but it must be said).

STATISTICAL DETAIL: Partial — no hypothesis test is performed (correctly, since this is a numerical-identity demonstration), so test/statistic/p-values are Not applicable. However the journal-mandated items are incomplete:
  - number of realizations/windows behind the averaged estimate in (a) and behind every point in (c) — absent
  - the estimator's degrees of freedom is given ($\nu=17$) but its definition is deferred to Section~\ref{sec:periodogram}, which breaks caption self-containment
  - no statement of the equivalent smoothing/resolution bandwidth ($2W=1$ Hz) or of whether $\nu$ is identical for all three routes (the central claim of the figure)

ABBREVIATIONS:      Undefined: EEG (expand to electroencephalogram/-graphy at first use); $L$ (used in the (c) axis, the $1/L$ guide line, and the caption text "window length" without being tied to the symbol $L$ or to the normalization $L/N$); $\lambda_k$ is named but the taper index range ($k=0,\dots,N-1$) is not given; $\nu$ named but not defined in-caption. PSD is defined correctly.

COLOR/SYMBOL KEY:   Present — in-figure keys in all three panels, mirrored in the caption. One mismatch: the caption calls the third trace "dashed purple" while the rendered color is pink/magenta, and the (b)/(c) traces use the same pink; harmonize the color words with the artwork (or use the in-figure labels verbatim).

SELF-CONTAINED:     No — (i) cross-reference to Section~\ref{sec:periodogram} for $\nu$; (ii) the dB reference level in (a) is unspecified (dB re what — $\mu$V$^2$/Hz? arbitrary?); (iii) the sliding-window construction is ambiguous: "a sinc window ... of length $16N$ slid across the record" cannot be reconciled with panel (c), where $L/N$ ranges 1–16, so the reader cannot tell whether $16N$ is the window length or the record length, nor what the record length is in (a); (iv) no statement of preprocessing (detrending/mean removal, filtering) or of the taper design parameter $NW=4$.

ISSUES:
  - [BLOCKING] Window/record lengths are internally inconsistent between the (a)/(b) description and panel (c). As written, a window of length $16N$ cannot be "slid one sample at a time" across a record of length $N=1024$, and $L$ is never defined. This makes the figure unreproducible from the caption.
  - [BLOCKING] Number of realizations/windows averaged is not stated for the "average" route in (a)–(b) or for any point in (c) — explicitly required by the journal for any averaged or distributional quantity.
  - [BLOCKING] Degrees of freedom are quoted but defined only by cross-reference; the caption must be readable without the main text. Also state whether $\nu=17$ applies to all three routes.
  - [ADVISORY] dB reference level / physical units of the PSD in (a) not given.
  - [ADVISORY] Absence of error bars not explained; state that the comparison is deterministic for a fixed record.
  - [ADVISORY] Color descriptor "purple" does not match the rendered pink/magenta; "thick grey" should be "thick grey" vs. figure's light grey band — verify.
  - [ADVISORY] Human-subject data ("de-identified scalp EEG from the authors' archive") — add the IRB/consent or exemption pointer, or state that it is a legacy de-identified record, per IEEE human-data policy.
  - [ADVISORY] Numerical claims in (b) ("$3\times10^{-13}$ dB", "0.06 dB") should be labelled as maxima over the plotted 0–40 Hz band, and the arithmetic precision (double, float64) noted, since $10^{-13}$ dB is at machine-precision level.
  - [ADVISORY] Preprocessing (mean removal/detrending), FFT length/zero-padding, and one-sided vs. two-sided convention unstated.
  - [ADVISORY] Panel (c) y-axis label appears to run to the top edge of the panel and the "(c)" label sits close to it; check at 88/181 mm final width that no glyph is clipped and that all text is ≥6 pt.

SUGGESTED FIXES:
  - Rewrite with explicit symbols and counts, e.g.: "\textbf{Three routes, one estimate.} (a) Power spectral density (PSD, dB re $\mu$V$^2$/Hz) of one channel of de-identified scalp EEG (8 s at 128 Hz, $N=1024$ samples, single epoch, mean removed; legacy de-identified record, IRB-exempt), computed with design half-bandwidth $W=4/N=0.5$ Hz ($NW=4$; equivalent resolution $2W=1$ Hz) by three routes: multitaper with all $N$ Slepian tapers weighted by eigenvalues $\lambda_k$, $k=0,\dots,N-1$ (thick grey); untapered periodogram convolved with a box of half-width $W$ (green); and the average of $M=\dots$ periodograms of a sinc window of half-bandwidth $W$ and length $L=16N$ slid one sample at a time along a record of length $\dots$ (dashed pink)."
  - Add to (b): "Absolute difference from the multitaper estimate, log scale; maxima over 0–40 Hz are $3\times10^{-13}$ dB (smoothed periodogram; at float64 round-off) and $0.06$ dB (sliding-window average)."
  - Add to (c): "Maximum absolute difference over 0–40 Hz between the sliding-window average and the multitaper estimate versus normalized window length $L/N$ ($L/N=1,2,4,8,16$; $M(L)=\dots$ windows averaged at each $L$; one epoch per point), with a $1/L$ reference line (dotted grey)."
  - Close with a self-contained degrees-of-freedom sentence: "All three routes have the same equivalent degrees of freedom, $\nu = 2\left(\sum_k \lambda_k\right)^2/\sum_k \lambda_k^2 = 17$ for this design; no error bars are shown because, for a fixed record, the three estimates are deterministic and the figure demonstrates their numerical equivalence rather than sampling variability."
  - Replace "purple" with the actual rendered color name and make the caption color words identical to the in-figure key strings.

### figure2.png

---
TITLE:              Present & bold ("Every estimator is the true spectrum blurred by a kernel, plus noise.")

PANEL DESCRIPTIONS: Complete — (a)–(d) and (e)–(h) are each described in order and individually identifiable; the grouped treatment ("(a--d)") is acceptable because each panel is then named explicitly.

ERROR BARS:         Not present — the figure shows a single realization per panel, not a distribution; no error-bar definition is required. The journal's companion requirement (number of realizations and estimator degrees of freedom) *is* met: "One realization ($N=1024$)" and "$\nu$ is the equivalent degrees of freedom."

SAMPLE SIZE:        Stated — one realization, N = 1024 samples. (Missing: what N denotes, i.e., record length in samples; and the number of realizations used for the kernel/"beyond 2W" computations, if any — these are deterministic, which should be said.)

STATISTICAL DETAIL: Partial — no hypothesis test is performed, so test statistic/p-values are not applicable. Absent: (i) the numerical ν values are only inside the figure, not in the caption; (ii) how ν was computed (e.g., ν = 2/∑w² for the smoother, ν = 2 for a single periodogram); (iii) the AR model order/coefficients and sampling convention behind the "true PSD."

ABBREVIATIONS:      Undefined: PSD (power spectral density); "dB re peak" (decibels relative to kernel maximum) used as an axis label; AR is written out ("autoregressive") but the model order is not given; "Fejér kernel" is used without stating it is the untapered (rectangular-window) spectral window. N and W are used quantitatively before being defined in words.

COLOR/SYMBOL KEY:   Missing (partially) — black = true PSD is stated, and the in-figure legend in (a) labels the grey trace "estimate (one realization)". However the orange traces in (b)–(d) and (f)–(h) are never identified in the caption or in any in-figure legend; a reader must infer that orange = estimate/kernel for tapered cases. Light-blue shading is defined ("blue shading marks |f| ≤ W") but the solid blue bar in (c)/(d) versus its absence in (a)/(b) is not explained. Grey filled region in (e)–(h) is defined.

SELF-CONTAINED:     No — the orange color coding, the meaning of N, the expansion of PSD, and the absence of the blue kernel-width bar in (a)–(b) must be inferred from the main text.

ISSUES:
  - [BLOCKING] Color key incomplete: the orange curves carry the primary result in six of eight panels and are nowhere defined. The only in-figure legend (panel a) shows a grey line labelled "estimate", which contradicts the orange traces used elsewhere.
  - [BLOCKING] Undefined abbreviation PSD on first use in the caption; IEEE TBME requires expansion at first occurrence in the legend unless already defined in the manuscript body (and captions must stand alone).
  - [BLOCKING] Estimator degrees of freedom are compared across panels (ν = 2.0, 2.0, 8.9, 50.0) but the ν values appear only inside the figure and the estimator for ν is not stated. The journal explicitly requires the estimator's degrees of freedom to be stated where noise levels are compared — put the numbers and the formula in the caption.
  - [ADVISORY] Each panel carries an in-figure title line ("no taper: leaky and noisy", "kernel: 1.3% beyond 2W", etc.). IEEE TBME asks for no titles inside the figure; these should be demoted to short axis-area annotations or moved into the caption.
  - [ADVISORY] The blue half-power-width bar is described as if present in all of (a)–(d) but is visible only in (c) and (d); in (a)/(b) it is sub-pixel. State this explicitly.
  - [ADVISORY] Panel labels (e), (g) collide with the adjacent in-figure title text ("(g)kernel: ..."), and the (a) legend entry "estimate (one realization)" overlaps the true-PSD line sample. Re-layout needed.
  - [ADVISORY] Reproducibility details missing: AR model order/coefficients, how the true PSD was evaluated, and how the "% beyond 2W" integral was computed (frequency grid, integration limits).
  - [ADVISORY] The dynamic range claim ("background spanning 65 dB") is not obviously verifiable from the plotted 10 dB-to-−20 dB range of the true PSD; either qualify it or state where the 65 dB is measured.
  - [ADVISORY] Axis label "kernel (dB re peak)" should be expanded in the caption for readers unfamiliar with "re".

SUGGESTED FIXES:
  - Add a color/line key sentence early: "Black, true power spectral density (PSD); grey, periodogram estimate (a) and Fejér kernel (e); orange, Hann-tapered estimates (b--d) and their kernels (f--h)." Alternatively add a single shared in-figure legend with both grey and orange entries.
  - Expand abbreviations on first use: "power spectral density (PSD)"; "$N=1024$ samples"; "decibels relative to the kernel maximum (dB re peak)"; and identify (e) as "the Fejér (rectangular-window) kernel".
  - State the noise/resolution statistics in the caption: "Equivalent degrees of freedom, computed as $\nu = 2/\sum_k w_k^2$ for the smoothed estimates and $\nu = 2$ for a single periodogram ordinate: $\nu = 2.0$ (a), 2.0 (b), 8.9 (c), 50.0 (d)."
  - Clarify the blue bar: "the short blue bar above the peaks is the half-power width of each estimator's kernel (too narrow to render in (a) and (b))."
  - Move the eight in-figure titles into the caption as parenthetical descriptors, e.g. "(a) no taper: leaky and noisy; (b) Hann taper: still noisy; ..." and delete them from the image, per IEEE TBME's no-in-figure-titles rule.
  - Add one methods sentence: "The process is an order-$p$ AR model with coefficients given in Section~X; the true PSD is evaluated analytically, and '% beyond $2W$' is the kernel power outside $|f|\le 2W$ computed on a 2^{16}-point frequency grid over $|f|\le 0.5$."
  - Fix the (g)/(e) label–text collisions and the panel-(a) legend overlap; verify all text is ≥6 pt at 181 mm double-column width and export as 600 DPI TIFF/EPS (RGB) for final submission.

All curves are exact numerical evaluations (scipy.signal.windows.dpss, double precision); no averaging or statistical estimation is involved, hence no error bars. (a) First six tapers (k = 0–5, colors per in-panel key), offset vertically by a constant for display; ordinate in arbitrary units. (b) Spectral windows |U_k(f)|² of the first eight unit-norm tapers (k = 0–7); successive windows fill the gaps left by lower orders inside the band (grey shading, |f| ≤ W). (c) Running sum Σ_{k<K} λ_k|U_k(f)|²/2NW for K = 1, 3, 5, 7, 9 (colors) and K = N (thick black); with all N tapers the sum is exactly the box of half-width W (dashed black, ideal box), i.e. Eq. (x); with the conventional K = 2NW−1 = 7 the box acquires rounded shoulders and ≲5 % in-band ripple. (d) Out-of-band energy 1−λ_k versus taper index k (k = 0–16, markers = computed eigenvalues, line for guidance; log ordinate). Orange dashed line: k = 2NW = 8; grey dotted line: retained K = 2NW−1 = 7. Beyond k = 2NW, 1−λ_k saturates near unity, so those tapers contribute only leakage."

  - [ADVISORY] (b) and (c) show only a subset of a continuum/of N members; caption says "four of a continuum shown" for (b) but not for (c), where only 4 of N sliding positions/eigen-tapers appear.

SUGGESTED FIXES:
  - Revised caption: "\textbf{Three ways to build a taper bank from a single window, compared with the Slepian reference bank.} All panels are deterministic constructions (no data, no averaging; hence no error bars). Record length $N=256$ samples; analysis half-bandwidth $W=4/N$ cycles/sample (time–bandwidth product $NW=4$). (a)–(d) Members of each bank; traces are vertically offset and arbitrarily scaled, so the amplitude axis is unitless. (a) Welch's method: seven time-shifted copies of one Hann window of segment length $L=N/4$ at 50\% overlap; colors index the seven offsets (left to right). (b) Hann-tapered periodogram smoothed by a Gaussian kernel of standard deviation $3/N$, equivalent to frequency-shifted copies of one taper; four representative shifts $f'=0,1,2,3$ cycles per $N$ samples are shown from a continuum (see in-figure key). (c) A sinc window of half-bandwidth $W$ slid one sample at a time; four of the $N$ positions are shown. In the long-window limit this equals the untapered periodogram smoothed with a box kernel, and equals Thomson's estimate using all $N$ Slepian tapers with weights equal to their eigenvalues $\lambda_k$ (the concentration ratios of the Slepian sequences). (d) Thomson's multitaper estimate with $K=7$ equally weighted Slepian tapers $k=0,\dots,3$ shown (see key). (e)–(h) Eigen-weight ladders $c_k$ of the banks in (a)–(d), respectively, normalized to unit sum and ordered by taper index $k$; the number of nonzero weights is 7, 12, 10 and 7. Ladder flatness sets the equivalent chi-square degrees of freedom $\nu=2(\sum_k c_k)^2/\sum_k c_k^2$ [Eq.~(\ref{eq:ladder})], giving $\nu=13.4$ (e), $11.1$ (f), $17.2$ (g) and $14.0$ (h). Bar color distinguishes methods only and carries no quantitative meaning. Eigen-tapers and smoothing kernels for all four banks are given in Fig.~\ref{fig:banksfull}."

  - Export as vector PDF/EPS (or ≥600 DPI TIFF) at 181 mm width, RGB, sans-serif 8–10 pt at final size, and verify no text falls below 6 pt.

### figure6.png

---
**TITLE:** Present & bold — "The everyday estimators on the record of Fig. \ref{fig:motif}" is bold and leads the legend. It is descriptive but leans on a cross-reference; a reader who has not seen Fig. motif learns nothing about *what* record this is.

**PANEL DESCRIPTIONS:** Complete — (a)–(d) and (e)–(h) are each addressed in order. However (e)–(h) are described only by the phrase "as in Fig. motif" plus interpretive commentary; the axes ("dB re peak", "frequency offset in units of 1/N") and the shaded bands are never explained here.

**ERROR BARS:** Not present — the figure shows one realization per method, and the caption says so explicitly ("one realization"). No error bars, bands, or distributions appear, so no error-bar definition is required. But see STATISTICAL DETAIL: the ν values printed in the panel titles are exactly the "estimator degrees of freedom" the journal requires to be stated when noise levels are compared, and they are undefined in the legend.

**SAMPLE SIZE:** Stated (partially) — N = 1024 samples and one realization are given; K = 7 tapers, nine Welch segments of 192 samples stepped by 104 are given. Missing: the number of realizations behind the median-absolute-error figures (if these are single-realization values they are not reproducible summary statistics; if they are averaged over realizations, the count must be stated), and the frequency band over which the median was taken.

**STATISTICAL DETAIL:** Partial — absent:
- Definition of ν (equivalent/effective degrees of freedom of each estimator) and how it was computed; this is the journal-mandated dof statement for the noise-level comparison across (a)–(d).
- Definition of the band used for "median absolute error … over the band".
- Definition of the kernel leakage metric "% beyond 2W" (fraction of kernel energy? of |kernel|? normalized how?).
- No dispersion or uncertainty on the 11.6 / 4.6 / 1.3 / 0.8 dB numbers, and no statement that these are single-realization values.
- No hypothesis test is used, which is defensible for a deterministic/illustrative comparison; state that no inferential test is performed so a reader does not look for one.

**ABBREVIATIONS:** Undefined: PSD (power spectral density); W (half-bandwidth) is used numerically but never named; NW (time–half-bandwidth product, panel b title); K stated as "tapers" only in passing — acceptable; "dB re peak" (decibels relative to kernel peak); "2W" band; Slepian and Fejér are named without definition (acceptable if defined earlier in the manuscript, but confirm).

**COLOR/SYMBOL KEY:** Present but inconsistent — the caption maps green/blue/orange/purple to the four methods, and panels (a) and (e) carry in-figure legends. Two problems: (i) the curve in (d)/(h) reads as pink/magenta, not purple, which will confuse readers and fails a grayscale check; (ii) the small blue tick mark above each peak in (a)–(d) (apparently a bandwidth/W marker) is drawn in all four panels in the same blue regardless of the method color and is never explained anywhere.

**SELF-CONTAINED:** No — a reader cannot decode ν, the "% beyond 2W" metric, the blue tick marks above the peaks, the error band, or the identity referenced as \eqref{eq:identity} without the main text.

**ISSUES:**
- [BLOCKING] ν appears in all four top-row panel titles and is the quantity that licenses the noise-level comparison between methods; it is never defined in the legend. IEEE TBME explicitly requires the estimator's degrees of freedom to be stated where noise levels are compared — define ν and its computation.
- [BLOCKING] The "median absolute error … over the band" has no band specified and no realization count. As written the numbers are unverifiable and, if single-realization, not stable statistics.
- [BLOCKING] The kernel leakage metric "% beyond 2W" in the (e)–(h) titles is the headline quantitative claim of the bottom row and is nowhere defined.
- [BLOCKING] The unexplained blue tick marks above the spectral peaks in (a)–(d) are undefined graphical elements.
- [BLOCKING] Effective resolution is ~292 DPI at 181 mm double-column width (2094 px), below the 300 DPI minimum for color line art and far below the 600 DPI preference. Re-export as vector (EPS/PDF) or at ≥600 DPI for the final submission; PNG is acceptable for review only.
- [ADVISORY] Color-name mismatch: caption says "purple", curve renders pink/magenta.
- [ADVISORY] The per-panel text above each axes block functions as an in-figure title, which the journal discourages. These read as method/parameter labels, which is usually tolerated, but consider moving the numeric claims (ν, % beyond 2W) into the caption and leaving only the short method label.
- [ADVISORY] The title's dependence on "the record of Fig. motif" makes the figure non-self-describing; add a one-clause description of the test process.
- [ADVISORY] PSD used in the in-figure legend ("true PSD") and in the caption without expansion.
- [ADVISORY] "the exact estimate \eqref{eq:identity}" — an equation cross-reference inside a caption breaks stand-alone readability; paraphrase in words.
- [ADVISORY] Panels (e)–(h) share a y-axis meaning but only (e) carries the shading legend; state in the caption that the gray/blue shading convention applies to all four.
- [ADVISORY] Confirm all in-figure text is ≥6 pt (≥8 pt preferred) after scaling to 181 mm; the axis tick labels are the smallest elements and should be checked at final size.

**SUGGESTED FIXES:**
- Add a dof sentence: "ν is the equivalent number of chi-square degrees of freedom of each estimator, computed as [formula/method]; ν = 17.2, 14.0, 8.9 and 17.6 for (a)–(d), so the four estimators are compared at matched variance."
- Specify the error statistic fully: "Median absolute deviation from the true PSD over f ∈ [f_lo, f_hi] cycles/sample for this single realization is 11.6, 4.6, 1.3 and 0.8 dB, respectively (one realization; values vary by ±X dB across R = N realizations)." Either give R or state plainly that these are single-realization values.
- Define the leakage metric: "Percentages in (e)–(h) give the fraction of total kernel energy falling outside |f| ≤ W (the shaded band)."
- Define the tick marks: "The short blue mark above each peak spans the estimator's half-power bandwidth 2W" (or whatever it denotes).
- Add abbreviation expansions on first use: "power spectral density (PSD)", "W = 4/N is the spectral half-bandwidth", "NW = 4 is the time–half-bandwidth product".
- Change "purple" to "pink" (or recolor panel (d)/(h) to a true purple) and verify the four colors remain distinguishable in grayscale and for deuteranopia.
- Add to the (e)–(h) sentence: "Gray shading shows the true spectrum (shape only, arbitrary vertical placement); the light blue band marks |f| ≤ W; kernels are plotted in dB relative to their own peak versus frequency offset in units of 1/N. Conventions in (e) apply to (f)–(h)."
- Add a one-line statement that no inferential statistical test is applied and the comparison is descriptive at matched ν.
- Replace "the exact estimate \eqref{eq:identity}" with a short verbal description, keeping the equation number in parentheses as a secondary pointer.
- Re-export the figure as EPS/PDF (vector) or ≥600 DPI TIFF at 181 mm width, RGB, under 10 MB.

ERROR BARS:         Not present — no error bars or shaded intervals are plotted; the caption instead quotes an analytic noise scale (4.3√(2/ν)), which is acceptable but must be labeled as such
SAMPLE SIZE:        Missing — the number of data records is implicit ("the EEG epoch of Fig. X", i.e. n = 1 epoch) but epoch duration, sampling rate, channel, subject, and the number of frequency bins entering the median/95th-percentile statistics are all unstated
STATISTICAL DETAIL: Partial — degrees of freedom ν are given for all three estimators (14, 8.9, 17.6) and half-power bandwidths are given; absent: (i) N of frequency bins behind the median |difference| and 95th-percentile values, (ii) frequency range over which those summaries were computed (0–40 Hz? excluding the <1 Hz region where the orange trace diverges by >15 dB?), (iii) definition/provenance of the 4.3√(2/ν) expression (asymptotic s.d. of a log-chi-square-distributed spectral estimate in dB), (iv) no hypothesis test is named — acceptable if none was performed, but say so explicitly ("no formal test; agreement assessed against the analytic estimator noise")
ABBREVIATIONS:      Undefined: EEG (spell out at first use in the caption); K (number of tapers — usable but not defined); W (the "same box" half-width is never given a numerical value in Hz); ν (state as "equivalent degrees of freedom"); "|diff|" in the in-figure legend of (b); "Slepian" side-lobe claim relies on DPSS terminology not introduced
COLOR/SYMBOL KEY:   Present — in-figure legends carry the full key; however the caption calls the third trace "purple" while the plotted line is pink/magenta (and (b) uses the same pink for "average minus multitaper"). Harmonize the colour words with the rendered hues
SELF-CONTAINED:     No — the signal itself is defined only by cross-reference ("the EEG epoch of Fig. \ref{fig:abstract}"); a reader cannot know epoch length, sampling rate, channel, or why harmonic peaks at ~1.35 Hz spacing are expected. The value of W is never given, and the number of frequency bins behind the median statistics is missing
ISSUES:
  - [BLOCKING] Journal rule: "State the number of realizations / windows behind any error bar or distribution." The median |difference| and 95th-percentile values in (b) are distribution summaries with no stated N of frequency bins and no stated frequency band. Add both.
  - [BLOCKING] No error-bar/uncertainty definition appears anywhere. Even though no bars are drawn, the caption asserts agreement "within their own noise" using 4.3√(2/ν); this quantity must be explicitly defined as the 1-σ log-spectral noise level in dB for a χ²_ν estimator, stated exactly once.
  - [BLOCKING] The smoothing half-width W is used in the in-figure legend and caption but never assigned a value; (a)'s orange curve cannot be reproduced or interpreted without it.
  - [BLOCKING] Caption is not self-contained: the data epoch is defined only by cross-reference. Restate epoch duration, sampling rate, channel/montage, and subject/dataset in one clause.
  - [ADVISORY] "purple" in the caption vs. pink/magenta as rendered; also verify colour-blind safety of the orange/pink pair, which are the two traces overlaid in (b).
  - [ADVISORY] EEG not spelled out in the caption.
  - [ADVISORY] The final sentence ("which the seventh Slepian taper's side lobes fill in") is a mechanistic claim, not an observation; either soften to "consistent with side-lobe leakage of the highest-order Slepian taper" or cite the supporting analysis.
  - [ADVISORY] The <1 Hz region where the Hann periodogram drops to −10 dB dominates the visual impression of (b) (a >15 dB excursion clipped by the axis). State whether this band was excluded from the median statistics; if the y-axis of (b) truncates a data excursion, say so.
  - [ADVISORY] Title sentence is a conclusion rather than a description; IEEE TBME prefers a brief descriptive caption. Consider "Comparison of multitaper, smoothed-periodogram, and Welch spectral estimates of a single EEG epoch."
  - [ADVISORY] Panel (b) y-axis label "difference from multitaper (dB)" is partially clipped at the top of the image canvas; check the exported bounding box.
  - [ADVISORY] Figure is 2113 px wide; at 181 mm double-column this is ~297 DPI, marginally below the 300 DPI minimum for colour line art (600 DPI preferred). Re-export as vector EPS/PDF.
SUGGESTED FIXES:
  - Re-export as vector PDF/EPS at 181 mm width (or raster ≥600 DPI) so the DPI requirement is met and the clipped (b) axis label is restored.
  - Recolour the third trace to a hue that matches the caption word, and verify the two-trace palette in (b) under deuteranopia simulation.
  - Replace the caption with a self-contained version, e.g.: "\textbf{Multitaper, smoothed-periodogram, and Welch estimates of one EEG epoch agree to within the estimators' own noise.} (a) Power spectral density of a single \SI{13.5}{s} epoch of electroencephalogram (EEG) from channel <X>, subject <Y>, sampled at \SI{250}{Hz} (n = 1 epoch; <M> frequency bins from 0 to \SI{40}{Hz}). Blue: Thomson multitaper with K = 7 Slepian (DPSS) tapers and equal weights, time-bandwidth NW = 4, equivalent degrees of freedom ν = 14.0. Orange: Hann-tapered periodogram smoothed with a rectangular box of half-width W = \SI{0.XX}{Hz}, ν = 8.9. Pink: Welch average of nine \SI{1.5}{s} Hann segments stepped by \SI{0.81}{s} (50\% overlap), ν = 17.6. Half-power bandwidths are 0.91, 1.03, and \SI{0.97}{Hz}, respectively. (b) Difference of the smoothed (orange) and Welch (pink) estimates from the multitaper estimate; the horizontal line marks zero. Over the <M> bins in <f_lo>–\SI{40}{Hz} the median absolute differences are 1.6 and \SI{0.9}{dB} and the 95th percentiles 4.9 and \SI{5.9}{dB}. For a χ²_ν-distributed estimate the 1-σ log-spectral noise level is $4.3\sqrt{2/\nu}$~dB, i.e. 1.6, 2.1, and \SI{1.5}{dB} for ν = 14.0, 8.9, and 17.6; the observed differences are therefore of the same order as the estimators' intrinsic variability. No hypothesis test was applied. Differences are largest in the valleys between harmonics, consistent with side-lobe leakage from the highest-order Slepian taper. Frequencies below \SI{1}{Hz} were excluded from the summary statistics because of the strong low-frequency bias of the single-taper estimate."
  - Add the numerical value of W and the multitaper time-bandwidth product NW to the in-figure legend of (a) so the panel is interpretable without the caption.
  - Expand "|diff|" in the (b) in-figure legend to "median |difference|" or define it in the caption.

### figure8.png

TITLE:              Present & bold — "Three estimators of a seizure." (concise and informative; note the in-figure panel headers duplicate this role and violate the journal's "no titles inside the figure" rule)
PANEL DESCRIPTIONS: Complete but out of order — panels are described as (a), (b), (c,e,g), (d,f), (h); IEEE convention is sequential (a)–(h). Panel (h)'s in-figure annotation ("median |diff| 1.5 dB") is never explained in the legend.
ERROR BARS:         Not present — no error bars, shaded CIs, or jackknife bands are drawn. However, the estimator degrees of freedom ν are stated for every estimator, which satisfies the journal's noise-level requirement.
SAMPLE SIZE:        Partial — "599 windows of 2 s stepped by 1 s" is stated (good), but the number of pixels behind the histogram in (h) is not given, and the number of frequency bins / NFFT is not given. The journal explicitly requires the number of realizations behind any distribution.
STATISTICAL DETAIL: Partial — no formal test is claimed (acceptable for a methods-demonstration figure), but the summary statistic shown in (h) (median |difference| = 1.5 dB) is not reported in the caption, and no dispersion measure (e.g., IQR or 5th–95th percentile of the difference) is given. No test name, statistic, dof, or p-value is needed if the comparison remains descriptive — but this should be explicit.
ABBREVIATIONS:      Undefined: EEG (expand at first use in caption); C4 (state "international 10–20 system"); NW (time–bandwidth product) and K (number of Slepian tapers); ν (state explicitly "ν = equivalent degrees of freedom of the spectral estimate"); dB (state the reference, e.g., dB re 1 µV²/Hz); "diff" in the (h) axis label; "ELROND seizure-annotation set" needs a citation/accession, not just a name.
COLOR/SYMBOL KEY:   Partial — the four-line key is drawn inside (f) only; the legend does not say it also applies to (d). The blue vs. orange dashed markers in (a) are not mapped to pre-ictal (t = 247 s) and ictal (t = 318 s); the orange trace in (b) is not linked to the orange marker; the white dashed lines in (c,e,g) are not mentioned; the shaded band colour in (a) is not identified as the annotated seizure interval.
SELF-CONTAINED:     No — the dB reference level, the pixel count for (h), the colour→instant mapping, the (h) in-figure median annotation, and the fact that the colour scale is shared with another figure (forcing the reader elsewhere) all block standalone interpretation.

  - [BLOCKING] The x-axis label of panel (h) is clipped at the right edge: "multitaper minus Hann-then-box (d" — the unit "(dB)" is cut off. Axis text must not be truncated.
  - [BLOCKING] No number of realizations is stated for the distribution in (h). Per journal policy, the count behind any distribution must be given (e.g., "n = 599 × 61 = 36 539 time–frequency pixels").
  - [BLOCKING] Power is reported in dB with no reference quantity anywhere in the figure or caption; the colour bar and all y-axes are therefore uninterpretable in absolute terms.
  - [BLOCKING] Each panel carries an in-figure title line (e.g., "scalp EEG, channel C4, common-average reference; seizure shaded", "multitaper, NW = 2, K = 3: ν = 6"). IEEE TBME prohibits titles inside the figure; this information must move to the caption or become axis/annotation text.
  - [ADVISORY] Panel descriptions are grouped non-sequentially (c,e,g then d,f then h); reorder to (a)–(h) or state explicitly "(c), (e), (g) share a format" at the start.
  - [ADVISORY] The in-figure statistic "median |diff| 1.5 dB" in (h) appears nowhere in the caption; a reader cannot tell whether it is the median absolute difference over all pixels or over a subset.
  - [ADVISORY] The line key exists only in (f); the legend must state that the same colours apply in (d).
  - [ADVISORY] Dashed-marker colours in (a) (blue, orange) and the orange trace in (b) are unmapped to the two instants; readers must guess which dashed line is pre-ictal.
  - [ADVISORY] White dashed vertical lines in (c), (e), (g) are undefined in the caption.
  - [ADVISORY] The colour scale is "shared with Fig. X" — this makes the figure dependent on another float. State the range (10–45 dB) as self-sufficient and mention the sharing only parenthetically.
  - [ADVISORY] Sampling rate (200 Hz) is given but no filter/notch settings, no NFFT, and no zero-padding statement; these determine the apparent smoothness of the spectra being compared.
  - [ADVISORY] "The channel is the one with the largest ictal rise in 2 to 20 Hz power" is a selection criterion buried in the last sentence; it belongs immediately after the channel is introduced in (a) and implies a selection over channels whose number is not given.
  - [ADVISORY] First x-tick (0 s) is absent on (c), (e), (g); axes begin visually at ~100 s, which is inconsistent with panel (a).

  - Fix the clipped (h) axis label and add units: "multitaper − Hann-then-box (dB)".
  - Delete the in-figure panel titles and fold their content into the caption.
  - Rewrite the caption in sequential panel order with the missing quantities, e.g.:
    "\textbf{Three spectral estimators applied to a focal seizure.} (a) Ten minutes of de-identified scalp electroencephalogram (EEG) sampled at 200 Hz (ELROND seizure-annotation set [ref]), channel C4 of the international 10–20 system referenced to the common average, amplitude clipped at ±500 µV. The peach band is the expert-annotated seizure interval; the blue dashed line marks the pre-ictal instant t = 247 s analysed in (d) and the orange dashed line the ictal instant t = 318 s analysed in (f). C4 was selected as the channel with the largest ictal increase in 2–20 Hz power among the N = ... recorded channels. (b) Six seconds of the ictal rhythm beginning at t = 318 s (orange, same instant as the orange marker in (a)). (c), (e), (g) Spectrograms computed from 599 Hann/Slepian-tapered windows of 2 s stepped by 1 s (NFFT = ..., no zero-padding): (c) single-taper Hann periodogram, equivalent degrees of freedom ν = 2; (e) multitaper estimate with time–bandwidth product NW = 2 and K = 3 Slepian tapers, ν = 6; (g) Hann periodogram smoothed across frequency with a boxcar of half-width W = 1 Hz, ν = 4.8. All three share the colour bar, 10–45 dB re 1 µV²/Hz (identical to Fig. X). White dashed lines mark the instants of (d) and (f). (d), (f) Spectra at the pre-ictal and ictal instants for the three estimators above plus Welch's method with 0.70-s Hann segments, 50 % overlap, ν = 7.7; the key in (f) applies to (d). (e) Histogram of the pixelwise difference (e) − (g) over all n = ... time–frequency pixels; median absolute difference 1.5 dB (IQR ... dB). No statistical test is applied; the comparison is descriptive, and the ν values quantify the noise level of each estimator."

  - Add the dB reference to the colour bar label and to the (d), (f) y-axis labels.

### figure9.png

---
TITLE:              Present & bold ("**Two seizures in one clip.**") — informative, though it labels content rather than the methodological comparison that is the figure's actual point

PANEL DESCRIPTIONS: Complete — (a) described individually; (b–d) described as a group but each estimator is named in the same order it appears, so the mapping is unambiguous

ERROR BARS:         Not present — appropriate for time–frequency images; the noise-level surrogate (equivalent degrees of freedom ν) is given for every panel, which satisfies the journal's estimator-DoF requirement

SAMPLE SIZE:        Partial — 599 windows of 2 s stepped by 1 s is stated (good, this is the "number of realizations behind the estimate"); missing: n = 1 subject / 1 clip / 1 channel, and the number of tapers averaged is given only implicitly via K = 3

STATISTICAL DETAIL: Not applicable — no hypothesis test is performed; no test statistic or p-value is required. However, the quantitative claims in the last sentence ("render it identically", "through noise") are asserted without any numeric support (no variance ratio, no SNR, no difference metric)

ABBREVIATIONS:      Undefined: EEG (spell out once: electroencephalogram/-graphy); Fp1 (state that it is a left prefrontal electrode of the international 10–20 system); ELROND (dataset/cohort name — must be expanded or cited at first use in the caption if the caption is to stand alone); NW (time–bandwidth product); K (number of Slepian/DPSS tapers); ν (equivalent degrees of freedom of the spectral estimator); W (smoother half-bandwidth — used in the in-figure title of (d) and only paraphrased as "half-width" in the caption); dB (reference quantity not given)

COLOR/SYMBOL KEY:   Partial — the shared colorbar is labelled "power (dB)" in-figure, but the caption does not state the dB reference (dB re 1 µV²/Hz?), the colormap name (viridis), or the fixed display limits (10–45 dB). The pale-orange shading in (a) is referred to only as "shaded"; the colour and its meaning (expert-marked seizure intervals) should be named, together with the onset/offset times or durations

SELF-CONTAINED:     No — (i) panels (b–d) are defined by cross-reference to another figure ("Spectrograms as in Fig. \ref{fig:eeg}"), so a reader cannot know the sampling rate, decimation/filtering, detrending, zero-padding/NFFT, or dB normalisation; (ii) ELROND is an opaque dataset label; (iii) the dB reference and colour limits are absent; (iv) no statement of how the seizure intervals were annotated or by whom

ISSUES:
  - [BLOCKING] Caption defers the entire spectrogram specification to another figure ("as in Fig. X"). IEEE TBME captions must be independently interpretable; at minimum restate sampling rate, taper/window type, NFFT, overlap, dB reference and colour limits.
  - [BLOCKING] The symbol ν, plus NW, K and W, appear in the figure and caption without definition. ν is the journal-mandated estimator degrees-of-freedom statistic and must be explicitly named as such in the caption.
  - [BLOCKING] Each of the four panels carries a text title inside the image ("scalp EEG, channel Fp1…", "Hann periodogram, 2-s windows: ν = 2", etc.). The target journal explicitly forbids titles inside the figure; this material belongs in the caption (or must be converted to short in-axes annotations such as "Hann, ν = 2").
  - [ADVISORY] "ELROND set" is undefined and non-standard; give the cohort/database name, institution, and ethics/IRB or consent statement for de-identified human EEG.
  - [ADVISORY] n is never stated at the subject level (n = 1 ten-minute clip, 1 channel, 1 patient). Readers may otherwise assume a grouped result.
  - [ADVISORY] Provenance of the shaded seizure intervals is unstated (expert consensus? single reader? number of reviewers?), as are the onset/offset times.
  - [ADVISORY] Shading colour is not named; in a greyscale printout the pale-orange band may be invisible, so also consider vertical dashed onset/offset markers.
  - [ADVISORY] "renders it identically" and "renders it through noise" are qualitative; supply a number (e.g., ratio of within-band log-power variance during the pre-ictal baseline across the three estimators) or soften the wording.
  - [ADVISORY] Panel (a) states clipping at ±500 µV; note the fraction of samples clipped, since the spikes visibly reach the axis limit and clipping alters the spectra in (b–d).
  - [ADVISORY] Panels (b–d) omit the amplitude/µV axis; the colorbar sits outside the (b) and (d) vertical extents, which reads as belonging to (c) alone — state in the caption that a single colour scale is shared by (b–d).
  - [ADVISORY) Check final-size type: at 181 mm width the long in-figure titles will fall near the 6 pt floor; removing them (per the blocking item above) resolves this.
  - [ADVISORY] Figure is PNG (accepted for review only). Supply TIFF/EPS/PDF at ≥300 DPI for the colour images at acceptance, and confirm file size <10 MB.

SUGGESTED FIXES:
  - Rewrite as, e.g.: "**Two seizures resolved by three spectral estimators with matched bandwidth.** (a) Ten minutes of de-identified scalp electroencephalogram (EEG) from one adult patient (n = 1 clip, 1 channel) of the ELROND corpus [ref], electrode Fp1 (left prefrontal, international 10–20 system) referenced to the common average, sampled at f_s = ___ Hz, displayed clipped at ±500 µV (___ % of samples clipped). Pale-orange bands mark the two electrographically confirmed seizures (onsets ___ s and ___ s; durations ___ s and ___ s) annotated independently by ___ reviewers. (b–d) Spectrograms of the same trace computed on 599 windows of 2 s stepped by 1 s (50 % overlap), NFFT = ___, power in dB re 1 µV²/Hz, shared colour scale 10–45 dB (viridis), plotted 0–30 Hz: (b) single-taper Hann periodogram, equivalent degrees of freedom ν = 2; (c) multitaper estimate with time–bandwidth product NW = 2 and K = 3 Slepian tapers, ν = 6; (d) Hann periodogram convolved in frequency with a boxcar of half-bandwidth W = 1.0 Hz, ν = 4.8. Each seizure appears as a harmonic stack descending from ≈20 Hz to ≈3 Hz over ≈120 s. The two ν ≳ 5 estimators ((c), (d)) give visually equivalent renderings (baseline log-power variance ___ and ___ dB²), whereas the ν = 2 periodogram (b) shows ___ dB² and resolves the stack only through speckle."
  - Delete the four in-figure titles and move their content verbatim into the caption; if an in-panel label is needed, use a compact right-aligned annotation ("Hann, ν = 2"; "multitaper, NW = 2, K = 3, ν = 6"; "Hann + 1 Hz box, ν = 4.8").
  - Define EEG, Fp1, NW, K, W, ν and the dB reference at first use in the caption; expand or cite ELROND and add the ethics/consent statement.
  - Centre the shared colorbar on the full (b)–(d) stack, or give each panel its own bar, and state explicitly in the caption that the scale is common to (b–d).
  - Add one quantitative noise metric per panel (e.g., standard deviation of log power in a pre-ictal baseline band) so the comparative claim is verifiable from the caption alone.
  - Export as TIFF/EPS at ≥300 DPI, RGB, 181 mm width, with all remaining text ≥6 pt at final size.

## Statistical Integrity Checker

### efigure1.png

---

**Context note:** This is a *deterministic analytical/simulation* figure (spectral concentration kernels computed from closed-form window functions at fixed $N$ and $W$). There is no sampled experimental data, no replicates, and no inferential comparison, so the distribution-visibility, sample-size, pairing, and multiple-comparison criteria are largely inapplicable. Review focuses on axis integrity, dynamic-range clipping, sampling/interpolation artifacts, and figure–caption quantitative consistency.

---
PLOT TYPE:          Appropriate — Line plots are the correct choice for continuous kernel functions of frequency offset; the dual linear/dB presentation is good practice because it shows the main lobe and the skirts, which cannot be read on a single scale. No bar/pie/error-bar constructs are used, so no distribution-hiding concern.
SAMPLE SIZE:        Not visible — Not a sampling-based figure, but the defining parameters are the analogue of $n$ here and only one is on-panel: $K = 2NW-1 = 7$ appears in the legend, while $N = 256$ and $W = 4/N$ appear only in the caption. Degrees of freedom ($\nu$) for each curve are caption-only. The figure is therefore not self-contained.
COMPARISON:         Valid — All five kernels are evaluated at the same design bandwidth and on a common abscissa, which is the correct matched comparison. Two concerns worth noting: (i) the normalizations differ between panels (panel (a) in "units of $N$", panel (b) re each curve's own peak), so a reader cannot carry a vertical reading from (a) into (b); (ii) panels (a) and (b) use different x-ranges ($\pm 10$ vs. $\pm 24$), which is defensible but should be made explicit so the narrower panel is not mistaken for the full support.
AXIS TRUNCATION:    Present — Panel (b) is floored at $-100$ dB and the Bohman curve runs off the bottom of the axis over roughly $|{\rm offset}| \gtrsim 13$, reappearing near $\pm 20$. The curve is clipped, not asymptotic, but it reads as if it terminates. The Hann curve also approaches the floor at the panel edges. This truncation understates the very dynamic range the caption is claiming, so it works against the authors rather than exaggerating their claim — but it is still an undisclosed clip. Panel (a) has no misleading truncation (baseline at 0.00), though the y-axis is labelled only to 0.10 while the multitaper ripple peaks near 0.13, leaving the top of the data in an untick-marked region.
OVERPLOTTING:       Present — Mild but consequential. Inside the passband in panel (a) the ideal box, Hann, and Bohman traces are nearly coincident and the dotted box is partially hidden beneath the solid curves; in panel (b) all five curves converge in the flat top and are indistinguishable. Additionally the palette uses two blues (dark blue = multitaper, light blue = Bohman) that are difficult to separate in greyscale or small-format print, and these two curves are exactly the pair a reader most needs to distinguish in panel (b).
MULTIPLE COMPARISONS: No concern — No hypothesis tests, p-values, or significance annotations are present; none are appropriate for deterministic kernels.
TIME SERIES:        Not applicable — The abscissa is frequency offset, not time. However, the analogous integrity issue *is* present: the visible staircase quantization on the Bohman and Hann skirts in panel (b) indicates the kernels were evaluated on a coarse frequency grid and then linearly interpolated, so the plotted skirt shape is partly a sampling artifact rather than the true kernel.
ISSUES:
  - [BLOCKING] Undisclosed clipping in panel (b): the Bohman curve exits the $-100$ dB floor and is not drawn over a substantial interval. A reader cannot tell whether the kernel is below $-100$ dB, undefined, or simply not computed there. Either extend the axis or state the floor and that curves are clipped.
  - [BLOCKING] Figure–caption quantitative mismatch: the caption states "the skirts differ by 40 dB," but at the panel edges the untapered curve sits near $-28$ dB while the Bohman curve is at or below $-90$ dB, i.e. a separation closer to 55–65 dB; near $\pm 10$ the separation is smaller. The stated single number is not readable from the plot at any offset. Specify the offset (or offset range) at which the 40 dB figure applies, or revise the number.
  - [ADVISORY] Staircase artifacts on the Hann and Bohman skirts suggest an under-resolved frequency grid with straight-line interpolation between evaluated points. The apparent skirt slope and any local minima in that region may be interpolation artifacts.
